# Crestline Algorithm Sensitivity Analysis Report

This report summarizes behavioral variations across parameter sweeps for `spur`, `tip`, and `radius_k` against default baselines.

## Executive Summary
- **Total Configurations Tested:** 2750
- **Matching Baseline (Pass):** 1316
- **Diverging Behavior (Fail/Sensitive):** 1434

## Failing (Sensitive) Configurations
The following parameter combinations produced outputs that diverged from the baseline:
